This paper describes and evaluates the sovereign credit ratings methodologies of Standard & Poor' s, Moody's Investors Service; and Fitch Ratings. A simple definition of ratings failure based on ratings stability_is proposed and tested, pointing to falling failure rates, consistent bias, and strong
